   1  0000              ; =========================================================================
   2  0000              ; Build NeoGS firmware and one deployable package from the Project 124 root:
   3  0000              ;   sjasmplus neogs_sub/build_firmware.asm
   4  0000              ;
   5  0000              ; neogs_code.bin       custom firmware, loaded at logical RAM address $0000
   6  0000              ; neogs_bootstrap.bin  intermediate bootstrap; neoprog.bin packages it with firmware

# file opened: main.asm
   1+ 0000              ; =========================================================================
   2+ 0000              ; PROJECT 124 — NeoGS Z80 firmware / command dispatcher
   3+ 0000              ; Based on NeoGS Programmer's Guide v0.1.A and ports.inc v0.3.
   4+ 0000              ;
   5+ 0000              ; MEMORY MAP / BOOT:
   6+ 0000              ; The stock GS105a ROM loads a tiny module at $5B00. That module enables
   7+ 0000              ; RAM-over-ROM and receives this firmware at logical RAM address $0000.
   8+ 0000              ; Firmware occupies fixed WIN0 ($0000-$3FFF). The Z80 stack is moved to fixed WIN2,
   9+ 0000              ; physical page #01, at $BFFE. x86_mem_map_addr remaps only WIN3 via MPAGEX;
  10+ 0000              ; MPAG/WIN2 remains fixed throughout guest-memory access. A 128-byte canary
  11+ 0000              ; at $B800-$B87F detects stack overflow; the usable stack area is ~1.87 KiB.
  12+ 0000              ; WIN1 remains on physical page #03; the DMA frame at logical $5000 maps to $00D000.
  13+ 0000              ; =========================================================================
  14+ 0000
  15+ 0000                      INCLUDE "../shared/neogs_io.inc"
# file opened: neogs_io.inc
   1++0000              ; =========================================================================
   2++0000              ; NeoGS ports.inc v0.3 — Project 124 used subset
   3++0000              ; Hardware port names/addresses from NedoPC ports.inc v0.3.
   4++0000              ; =========================================================================
   5++0000
   6++0000              ; ZX Spectrum side
   7++0000              GSCOM           EQU #BB             ; write command / read status
   8++0000              GSSTAT          EQU #BB             ; read CBIT (bit 0), DBIT (bit 7)
   9++0000              GSDAT           EQU #B3             ; read/write data byte
  10++0000              GSCTR           EQU #33             ; global control
  11++0000
  12++0000              B_CBIT          EQU 0
  13++0000              M_CBIT          EQU #01
  14++0000              B_DBIT          EQU 7
  15++0000              M_DBIT          EQU #80
  16++0000
  17++0000              C_GRST          EQU #80
  18++0000              C_GNMI          EQU #40
  19++0000              C_GLED          EQU #20
  20++0000
  21++0000              ; NeoGS Z80 local ports
  22++0000              MPAG            EQU #00
  23++0000              MPAGEX          EQU #10             ; independent 16 KiB page for C000-FFFF
  24++0000              ZXCMD           EQU #01
  25++0000              ZXDATRD         EQU #02
  26++0000              ZXDATWR         EQU #03
  27++0000              ZXSTAT          EQU #04
  28++0000              CLRCBIT         EQU #05
  29++0000
  30++0000              GSCFG0          EQU #0F
  31++0000              B_NOROM         EQU 0
  32++0000              M_NOROM         EQU #01
  33++0000              B_RAMRO         EQU 1
  34++0000              M_RAMRO         EQU #02
  35++0000              B_8CHANS        EQU 2
  36++0000              M_8CHANS        EQU #04
  37++0000              B_EXPAG         EQU 3
  38++0000              M_EXPAG         EQU #08
  39++0000              B_CKSEL0        EQU 4
  40++0000              M_CKSEL0        EQU #10
  41++0000              B_CKSEL1        EQU 5
  42++0000              M_CKSEL1        EQU #20
  43++0000              C_10MHZ         EQU #30
  44++0000              C_12MHZ         EQU #10
  45++0000              C_20MHZ         EQU #20
  46++0000              C_24MHZ         EQU #00
  47++0000              B_PAN4CH        EQU 6
  48++0000              M_PAN4CH        EQU #40
  49++0000              B_INV7B         EQU 7
  50++0000              M_INV7B         EQU #80
  51++0000
  52++0000              ; NeoGS physical page registers
  53++0000              PG0             EQU #20
  54++0000              PG1             EQU #21
  55++0000              PG2             EQU #22
  56++0000              PG3             EQU #23
  57++0000
  58++0000              ; NeoGS DMA registers
  59++0000              DMA_MOD         EQU #1B
  60++0000              DMA_HAD         EQU #1C
  61++0000              DMA_MAD         EQU #1D
  62++0000              DMA_LAD         EQU #1E
  63++0000              DMA_CST         EQU #1F
  64++0000              C_DMA_NONE      EQU 0
  65++0000              C_DMA_ZX        EQU 1
  66++0000              C_DMA_SD        EQU 2
  67++0000              C_DMA_MP3       EQU 3
  68++0000
  69++0000              ; Standard General Sound ROM commands.
  70++0000              GS_CMD_RESET_FLAGS  EQU #00
  71++0000              GS_CMD_GET_TOTAL_RAM EQU #20
  72++0000              GS_CMD_LOAD_MODULE  EQU #30
  73++0000              GS_CMD_RUN_MODULE   EQU #31
  74++0000              GS_CMD_OPEN_STREAM  EQU #D1
  75++0000              GS_CMD_CLOSE_STREAM EQU #D2
  76++0000              GS_CMD_COLD_RESTART EQU #F4
  77++0000              GS_CMD_JUMP_ADDRESS EQU #13
  78++0000              GS_CMD_RECEIVE_BLOCK EQU #14
  79++0000
  80++0000              ; Project 124 private commands carried by GSCOM after custom firmware starts.
  81++0000              CMD_RESET       EQU #01
  82++0000              CMD_LOAD_X86    EQU #02
  83++0000              CMD_RUN_X86     EQU #03
  84++0000              CMD_GET_STATUS  EQU #04
  85++0000              CMD_MEM_TEST    EQU #05
  86++0000              CMD_BIOS_POST   EQU #07
  87++0000              CMD_INT_TEST    EQU #08
  88++0000              CMD_INT_GUEST_TEST EQU #09
  89++0000              CMD_BIOS_VIDEO_TEST EQU #0A
  90++0000              CMD_RUN_X86_REAL EQU #0B
  91++0000
  92++0000              ; Project 124 startup handshake bytes.
  93++0000              NGS_BOOTSTRAP_READY_ACK EQU #A1
  94++0000              NGS_BOOTSTRAP_LENGTH_ACK EQU #A3
  95++0000              NGS_FIRMWARE_READY_ACK  EQU #A2
  96++0000              NGS_BOOTSTRAP_ERROR_ACK EQU #E1
  97++0000              NGS_RESULT_SIGNATURE   EQU #A5
  98++0000
  99++0000              NGS_STATUS_CBIT EQU M_CBIT
 100++0000              NGS_STATUS_DBIT EQU M_DBIT
 101++0000
 102++0000              ; The proven x86 DMA frame occupies logical $5000..$7FFF (12 KiB).
 103++0000              ; Default NeoGS WIN1 page #03 maps logical $5000 to physical $00D000.
 104++0000              ; The two-byte length header leaves #2FFE bytes for the guest image.
 105++0000              NGS_X86_DMA_WINDOW_SIZE EQU #3000
 106++0000              NGS_X86_MAX_CODE_SIZE   EQU #2FFE

  16+ 0000
  17+ 0000                      ORG     $0000
  18+ 0000
  19+ 0000              ROM_RETURN_TRAMPOLINE_ADDR EQU $4000
  20+ 0000              X86_DMA_HEADER_ADDR        EQU $5000
  21+ 0000              X86_RAM_RUN_ADDR           EQU $5002
  22+ 0000              Z80_STACK_TOP              EQU $BFFE
  23+ 0000              Z80_STACK_GUARD_BASE       EQU $B800
  24+ 0000              Z80_STACK_GUARD_SIZE       EQU $0080
  25+ 0000              Z80_STACK_FILL_LENGTH      EQU Z80_STACK_TOP - Z80_STACK_GUARD_BASE - 1
  26+ 0000              DMA_WAIT_LIMIT   EQU #FFFF
  27+ 0000              DMA_WAIT_OUTER   EQU 8
  28+ 0000              IPC_WAIT_LIMIT   EQU #FFFF
  29+ 0000
  30+ 0000              ; Firmware entry vector
  31+ 0000 C3 00 01             jp      start_neoprogram
  32+ 0003 00 00 00...          BLOCK   53, 0
  33+ 0038 FB                   ei
  34+ 0039 C9                   ret
  35+ 003A 00 00 00...          BLOCK   198, 0
  36+ 0100
  37+ 0100              start_neoprogram:
  38+ 0100 F3                   di
  39+ 0101 ED 56                im      1
  40+ 0103 31 FE BF             ld      sp, Z80_STACK_TOP     ; fixed WIN2/page #01; only WIN3 is remapped by MPAGEX
  41+ 0106                      ; Fill the reserved stack area with a canary before the first CALL/PUSH.
  42+ 0106                      ; The lower 128 bytes remain a guard; normal stack use grows down from BFFE.
  43+ 0106 21 00 B8             ld      hl, Z80_STACK_GUARD_BASE
  44+ 0109 36 A5                ld      (hl), #A5
  45+ 010B 11 01 B8             ld      de, Z80_STACK_GUARD_BASE + 1
  46+ 010E 01 FD 07             ld      bc, Z80_STACK_FILL_LENGTH
  47+ 0111 ED B0                ldir
  48+ 0113
  49+ 0113                      ; Set Z80 to the documented maximum guaranteed clock: 20 MHz.
  50+ 0113                      ; Do not select 24 MHz: the guide explicitly says it is not guaranteed.
  51+ 0113 DB 0F                in      a, (GSCFG0)
  52+ 0115 E6 CF                and     #CF
  53+ 0117 F6 20                or      C_20MHZ
  54+ 0119 D3 0F                out     (GSCFG0), a
  55+ 011B
  56+ 011B AF                   xor     a
  57+ 011C 32 29 04             ld      (neo_dma_error), a
  58+ 011F 32 2A 04             ld      (saved_z80_ax), a
  59+ 0122 32 2B 04             ld      (saved_z80_ax+1), a
  60+ 0125 32 2C 04             ld      (saved_x86_ax), a
  61+ 0128 32 2D 04             ld      (saved_x86_ax+1), a
  62+ 012B 32 04 0D             ld      (x86_exit_status), a
  63+ 012E 32 27 04             ld      (neo_command), a
  64+ 0131 32 28 04             ld      (neo_tx_byte), a
  65+ 0134
  66+ 0134                      ; Copy the ROM-return trampoline into unoverlaid RAM at $4000.
  67+ 0134 21 2E 04             ld      hl, rom_return_trampoline_code
  68+ 0137 11 00 40             ld      de, ROM_RETURN_TRAMPOLINE_ADDR
  69+ 013A 01 0C 00             ld      bc, rom_return_trampoline_end - rom_return_trampoline_code
  70+ 013D ED B0                ldir
  71+ 013F
  72+ 013F                      ; Notify the Spectrum only after the new firmware is executing.
  73+ 013F 3E A2                ld      a, NGS_FIRMWARE_READY_ACK
  74+ 0141 CD FA 03             call    neo_send_data_byte
  75+ 0144
  76+ 0144              main_loop:
  77+ 0144                      ; A command exists only while CBIT (ZXSTAT bit 0) is set.
  78+ 0144 DB 04                in      a, (ZXSTAT)
  79+ 0146 CB 47                bit     B_CBIT, a
  80+ 0148 28 FA                jr      z, main_loop
  81+ 014A
  82+ 014A                      ; Read the command byte from ZXCMD. Reading it does NOT clear CBIT.
  83+ 014A DB 01                in      a, (ZXCMD)
  84+ 014C 32 27 04             ld      (neo_command), a
  85+ 014F
  86+ 014F                      ; Standard GS #F4 cold restart also returns this custom firmware to ROM.
  87+ 014F FE F4                cp      GS_CMD_COLD_RESTART
  88+ 0151 CA 8C 01             jp      z, command_return_to_gsrom
  89+ 0154 FE 01                cp      CMD_RESET
  90+ 0156 CA 94 01             jp      z, command_reset
  91+ 0159 FE 02                cp      CMD_LOAD_X86
  92+ 015B CA B1 01             jp      z, command_load_x86
  93+ 015E FE 03                cp      CMD_RUN_X86
  94+ 0160 CA 01 02             jp      z, command_run_x86
  95+ 0163 FE 04                cp      CMD_GET_STATUS
  96+ 0165 CA 74 03             jp      z, command_get_status
  97+ 0168 FE 05                cp      CMD_MEM_TEST
  98+ 016A CA 18 03             jp      z, command_mem_test
  99+ 016D FE 07                cp      CMD_BIOS_POST
 100+ 016F CA 21 03             jp      z, command_bios_post
 101+ 0172 FE 08                cp      CMD_INT_TEST
 102+ 0174 CA 2A 03             jp      z, command_int_test
 103+ 0177 FE 09                cp      CMD_INT_GUEST_TEST
 104+ 0179 CA 33 03             jp      z, command_int_guest_test
 105+ 017C FE 0A                cp      CMD_BIOS_VIDEO_TEST
 106+ 017E CA 3C 03             jp      z, command_bios_video_test
 107+ 0181 FE 0B                cp      CMD_RUN_X86_REAL
 108+ 0183 CA 45 03             jp      z, command_run_x86_real
 109+ 0186
 110+ 0186                      ; Unknown command: release CBIT so the host cannot deadlock.
 111+ 0186 AF                   xor     a
 112+ 0187 D3 05                out     (CLRCBIT), a
 113+ 0189 C3 44 01             jp      main_loop
 114+ 018C
 115+ 018C              ; A #F4 command is also the standard GS105a cold-restart command.
 116+ 018C              ; If our firmware is running, reveal the stock ROM again and jump to its
 117+ 018C              ; reset vector. Leave CBIT asserted: the ROM's cold-start path clears it when
 118+ 018C              ; the standard firmware is ready, so the host's long wait is a real handshake.
 119+ 018C              command_return_to_gsrom:
 120+ 018C 3E 03                ld      a, 3
 121+ 018E CD 24 04             call    neo_set_pg1             ; restore trampoline's physical page
 122+ 0191 C3 00 40             jp      ROM_RETURN_TRAMPOLINE_ADDR
 123+ 0194
 124+ 0194              command_reset:
 125+ 0194 3E 03                ld      a, #03
 126+ 0196 CD 24 04             call    neo_set_pg1             ; ensure firmware page 1 is visible
 127+ 0199 AF                   xor     a
 128+ 019A 32 29 04             ld      (neo_dma_error), a
 129+ 019D 32 04 0D             ld      (x86_exit_status), a
 130+ 01A0 32 2A 04             ld      (saved_z80_ax), a
 131+ 01A3 32 2B 04             ld      (saved_z80_ax+1), a
 132+ 01A6 32 2C 04             ld      (saved_x86_ax), a
 133+ 01A9 32 2D 04             ld      (saved_x86_ax+1), a
 134+ 01AC D3 05                out     (CLRCBIT), a
 135+ 01AE C3 44 01             jp      main_loop
 136+ 01B1
 137+ 01B1              ; -------------------------------------------------------------------------
 138+ 01B1              ; CMD_LOAD_X86
 139+ 01B1              ; 1) Keep default WIN1 page #03 selected; logical $5000 maps to $00D000.
 140+ 01B1              ; 2) Select ZX DMA module and set physical DMA target $00D000.
 141+ 01B1              ; 3) Enable DMA by setting DMA_CST bit 7.
 142+ 01B1              ; 4) Clear CBIT: this is the READY indication to the host.
 143+ 01B1              ; 5) Wait for host's end marker (#FF written to GSCOM, setting CBIT).
 144+ 01B1              ; 6) Disable DMA, then clear CBIT to acknowledge completion.
 145+ 01B1              ;
 146+ 01B1              ; Host uses OUT/LDIR writes into $0000-$3FFF. No dummy write is performed.
 147+ 01B1              ; -------------------------------------------------------------------------
 148+ 01B1              command_load_x86:
 149+ 01B1                      ; Keep the documented default WIN1 page selected. DMA writes to
 150+ 01B1                      ; physical $00D000, which appears at logical $5000 in page #03.
 151+ 01B1 3E 03                ld      a, #03
 152+ 01B3 CD 24 04             call    neo_set_pg1
 153+ 01B6
 154+ 01B6 3E 01                ld      a, C_DMA_ZX
 155+ 01B8 D3 1B                out     (DMA_MOD), a
 156+ 01BA
 157+ 01BA AF                   xor     a
 158+ 01BB D3 1C                out     (DMA_HAD), a            ; physical address bits 21:16 = 0
 159+ 01BD 3E D0                ld      a, #D0
 160+ 01BF D3 1D                out     (DMA_MAD), a            ; physical address bits 15:8 = #D0
 161+ 01C1 AF                   xor     a
 162+ 01C2 D3 1E                out     (DMA_LAD), a            ; physical address bits 7:0 = 0
 163+ 01C4
 164+ 01C4 3E 80                ld      a, #80
 165+ 01C6 D3 1F                out     (DMA_CST), a            ; DMA enable = bit 7
 166+ 01C8
 167+ 01C8 AF                   xor     a
 168+ 01C9 D3 05                out     (CLRCBIT), a            ; host may now start LDIR
 169+ 01CB
 170+ 01CB 16 08                ld      d, DMA_WAIT_OUTER
 171+ 01CD              .dma_wait_outer:
 172+ 01CD 01 FF FF             ld      bc, DMA_WAIT_LIMIT
 173+ 01D0              .dma_wait_host_end:
 174+ 01D0 DB 04                in      a, (ZXSTAT)
 175+ 01D2 CB 47                bit     B_CBIT, a
 176+ 01D4 20 1F                jr      nz, .dma_host_end
 177+ 01D6 0B                   dec     bc
 178+ 01D7 78                   ld      a, b
 179+ 01D8 B1                   or      c
 180+ 01D9 20 F5                jr      nz, .dma_wait_host_end
 181+ 01DB 15                   dec     d
 182+ 01DC 20 EF                jr      nz, .dma_wait_outer
 183+ 01DE
 184+ 01DE                      ; Timeout: close DMA, restore PG1 and release the host command line.
 185+ 01DE AF                   xor     a
 186+ 01DF D3 1F                out     (DMA_CST), a
 187+ 01E1 D3 05                out     (CLRCBIT), a
 188+ 01E3 3E 03                ld      a, #03
 189+ 01E5 CD 24 04             call    neo_set_pg1             ; restore page 1 on DMA timeout
 190+ 01E8 3E 01                ld      a, 1
 191+ 01EA 32 29 04             ld      (neo_dma_error), a
 192+ 01ED 3E 03                ld      a, 3
 193+ 01EF 32 04 0D             ld      (x86_exit_status), a
 194+ 01F2 C3 44 01             jp      main_loop
 195+ 01F5
 196+ 01F5              .dma_host_end:
 197+ 01F5 AF                   xor     a
 198+ 01F6 D3 1F                out     (DMA_CST), a            ; disable DMA before leaving handler
 199+ 01F8 D3 05                out     (CLRCBIT), a            ; acknowledge host's #FF marker
 200+ 01FA AF                   xor     a
 201+ 01FB 32 29 04             ld      (neo_dma_error), a
 202+ 01FE C3 44 01             jp      main_loop
 203+ 0201              neogs_dma_handler_end:
 204+ 0201
 205+ 0201              ; -------------------------------------------------------------------------
 206+ 0201              ; CMD_RUN_X86 — execute the length-framed payload beginning at $5002.
 207+ 0201              ; -------------------------------------------------------------------------
 208+ 0201              command_run_x86:
 209+ 0201 CD 0F 02             call    execute_neogs_x86
 210+ 0204 3E 03                ld      a, #03
 211+ 0206 CD 24 04             call    neo_set_pg1             ; restore firmware/trampoline page
 212+ 0209 AF                   xor     a
 213+ 020A D3 05                out     (CLRCBIT), a
 214+ 020C C3 44 01             jp      main_loop
 215+ 020F
 216+ 020F              execute_neogs_x86:
 217+ 020F 3A 29 04             ld      a, (neo_dma_error)
 218+ 0212 B7                   or      a
 219+ 0213 28 0C                jr      z, .dma_ok
 220+ 0215 21 FF FF             ld      hl, $FFFF
 221+ 0218 22 2C 04             ld      (saved_x86_ax), hl
 222+ 021B 3E 03                ld      a, 3
 223+ 021D 32 04 0D             ld      (x86_exit_status), a
 224+ 0220 C9                   ret
 225+ 0221
 226+ 0221              .dma_ok:
 227+ 0221                      ; Emit a live NeoGS-side dump before interpreting the DMA payload.
 228+ 0221                      ; The host drains this stream and mirrors it to emulator port #EF.
 229+ 0221 CD 9E 03             call    neo_debug_dump_dma_frame
 230+ 0224
 231+ 0224                      ; DMA frame begins with a little-endian x86 byte length at $5000.
 232+ 0224 2A 00 50             ld      hl, (X86_DMA_HEADER_ADDR)
 233+ 0227 7C                   ld      a, h
 234+ 0228 B5                   or      l
 235+ 0229 CA 5E 02             jp      z, .invalid_x86_length
 236+ 022C
 237+ 022C                      ; Maximum code length is $2FFE: $5002 + length must not exceed $8000.
 238+ 022C 7C                   ld      a, h
 239+ 022D FE 2F                cp      #2F
 240+ 022F 38 09                jr      c, .x86_length_valid
 241+ 0231 C2 5E 02             jp      nz, .invalid_x86_length
 242+ 0234 7D                   ld      a, l
 243+ 0235 FE FF                cp      #FF
 244+ 0237 D2 5E 02             jp      nc, .invalid_x86_length
 245+ 023A
 246+ 023A              .x86_length_valid:
 247+ 023A 11 02 50             ld      de, X86_RAM_RUN_ADDR
 248+ 023D 19                   add     hl, de
 249+ 023E 22 02 0D             ld      (x86_program_end), hl
 250+ 0241
 251+ 0241 21 00 00             ld      hl, 0
 252+ 0244 22 2A 04             ld      (saved_z80_ax), hl
 253+ 0247 21 FF FF             ld      hl, #FFFF
 254+ 024A 22 00 0D             ld      (x86_steps_remaining), hl
 255+ 024D AF                   xor     a
 256+ 024E 32 04 0D             ld      (x86_exit_status), a
 257+ 0251
 258+ 0251 01 02 50             ld      bc, X86_RAM_RUN_ADDR
 259+ 0254 CD 45 0A             call    x86_execute_loop
 260+ 0257
 261+ 0257 2A 2A 04             ld      hl, (saved_z80_ax)
 262+ 025A 22 2C 04             ld      (saved_x86_ax), hl
 263+ 025D C9                   ret
 264+ 025E
 265+ 025E              .invalid_x86_length:
 266+ 025E 21 FF FF             ld      hl, #FFFF
 267+ 0261 22 2A 04             ld      (saved_z80_ax), hl
 268+ 0264 22 2C 04             ld      (saved_x86_ax), hl
 269+ 0267 3E 04                ld      a, 4
 270+ 0269 32 04 0D             ld      (x86_exit_status), a
 271+ 026C C9                   ret
 272+ 026D
 273+ 026D              ; Execute the loaded DMA task as bounded guest code at 0000:5002.
 274+ 026D              ; The DMA frame is copied into the x86 physical-memory backend before execution.
 275+ 026D              execute_neogs_x86_real:
 276+ 026D                      ; Match the legacy loader's DMA error and length validation.
 277+ 026D 3A 29 04             ld      a, (neo_dma_error)
 278+ 0270 B7                   or      a
 279+ 0271 28 0D                jr      z, .real_dma_ok
 280+ 0273 21 FF FF             ld      hl, #FFFF
 281+ 0276 22 2C 04             ld      (saved_x86_ax), hl
 282+ 0279 3E 03                ld      a, 3
 283+ 027B 32 04 0D             ld      (x86_exit_status), a
 284+ 027E 37                   scf
 285+ 027F C9                   ret
 286+ 0280
 287+ 0280              .real_dma_ok:
 288+ 0280 2A 00 50             ld      hl, (X86_DMA_HEADER_ADDR)
 289+ 0283 7C                   ld      a, h
 290+ 0284 B5                   or      l
 291+ 0285 CA FE 02             jp      z, .real_invalid_length
 292+ 0288 7C                   ld      a, h
 293+ 0289 FE 2F                cp      #2F
 294+ 028B 38 09                jr      c, .real_length_valid
 295+ 028D C2 FE 02             jp      nz, .real_invalid_length
 296+ 0290 7D                   ld      a, l
 297+ 0291 FE FF                cp      #FF
 298+ 0293 D2 FE 02             jp      nc, .real_invalid_length
 299+ 0296
 300+ 0296              .real_length_valid:
 301+ 0296 11 02 50             ld      de, X86_RAM_RUN_ADDR
 302+ 0299 19                   add     hl, de
 303+ 029A 22 61 18             ld      (x86_real_task_end), hl
 304+ 029D
 305+ 029D CD 3A 04             call    x86_mem_init
 306+ 02A0 21 02 50             ld      hl, X86_RAM_RUN_ADDR
 307+ 02A3 11 02 50             ld      de, #5002
 308+ 02A6 ED 4B 00 50          ld      bc, (X86_DMA_HEADER_ADDR)
 309+ 02AA CD A4 10             call    x86_real_copy_image
 310+ 02AD DA 0B 03             jp      c, .real_copy_failed
 311+ 02B0
 312+ 02B0                      ; Reset the guest register file to a deterministic real-mode entry.
 313+ 02B0 21 00 00             ld      hl, 0
 314+ 02B3 22 1F 0A             ld      (x86_reg_ax), hl
 315+ 02B6 22 25 0A             ld      (x86_reg_bx), hl
 316+ 02B9 22 21 0A             ld      (x86_reg_cx), hl
 317+ 02BC 22 23 0A             ld      (x86_reg_dx), hl
 318+ 02BF 22 29 0A             ld      (x86_reg_bp), hl
 319+ 02C2 22 2B 0A             ld      (x86_reg_si), hl
 320+ 02C5 22 2D 0A             ld      (x86_reg_di), hl
 321+ 02C8 22 2F 0A             ld      (x86_reg_es), hl
 322+ 02CB 22 35 0A             ld      (x86_reg_ds), hl
 323+ 02CE 22 31 0A             ld      (x86_reg_cs), hl
 324+ 02D1 21 02 50             ld      hl, #5002
 325+ 02D4 22 37 0A             ld      (x86_reg_ip), hl
 326+ 02D7 21 00 70             ld      hl, #7000
 327+ 02DA 22 33 0A             ld      (x86_reg_ss), hl
 328+ 02DD 21 00 01             ld      hl, #0100
 329+ 02E0 22 27 0A             ld      (x86_reg_sp), hl
 330+ 02E3 21 02 02             ld      hl, #0202
 331+ 02E6 22 39 0A             ld      (x86_reg_flags), hl
 332+ 02E9
 333+ 02E9 3E 01                ld      a, 1
 334+ 02EB 32 60 18             ld      (x86_real_bounds_enable), a
 335+ 02EE CD 33 11             call    x86_real_execute
 336+ 02F1 F5                   push    af

 526+ 03FA              ; -------------------------------------------------------------------------
 527+ 03FA              ; neo_send_data_byte
 528+ 03FA              ; IN: A = byte to send to Spectrum.
 529+ 03FA              ; Wait for DBIT=0, write ZXDATWR (#03), then wait for host read to clear DBIT.
 530+ 03FA              ; OUT: carry clear on success, carry set on timeout. Preserves HL and DE.
 531+ 03FA              ; -------------------------------------------------------------------------

 582+ 042E              ; but the following JP is still fetched from RAM because PC remains in $4000.

 592+ 043A                      INCLUDE "x86_memory.asm"
# file opened: x86_memory.asm
   1++043A              ; =========================================================================
   2++043A              ; PROJECT 124 — 8086 physical memory backend for NeoGS GS105a
   3++043A              ;
   4++043A              ; x86 physical $00000-$FFFFF maps to NeoGS RAM pages 4..67
   5++043A              ; (NeoGS physical base $010000, page size $4000 / 16 KiB).
   6++043A              ; Window C000-FFFF is switched through MPAGEX. Firmware code remains in fixed
   7++043A              ; WIN0/page 0; the Z80 stack is in fixed WIN2/page 1 at BFFE. MPAGEX never
   8++043A              ; changes WIN2, so this stack remains safe during x86 RAM accesses. The DMA
   9++043A              ; frame is in WIN1/page 3.
  10++043A              ;
  11++043A              ; x86_mem_addr is a 20-bit little-endian address:
  12++043A              ;   +0 = bits 7:0, +1 = bits 15:8, +2 = bits 19:16 (valid 0..0F).
  13++043A              ;
  14++043A              ; API:
  15++043A              ;   x86_mem_init: enable NOROM + EXPAG, preserving other GSCFG0 bits.
  16++043A              ;   x86_mem_read8:  IN x86_mem_addr; OUT A=value, carry=invalid address.
  17++043A              ;   x86_mem_write8: IN x86_mem_addr and x86_mem_value; carry=invalid address.
  18++043A              ;   x86_mem_test: destructive test of the reserved 1 MiB x86 RAM region.
  19++043A              ;                 status=0, AX=0001 on pass; status=5, AX=phase:page on fail.
  20++043A              ;
  21++043A              ; The test leaves NOROM+EXPAG enabled. Only window 3 is switched by MPAGEX;
  22++043A              ; callers must keep SP below C000 for the entire test (Project124 uses BFFE).
  23++043A              ; =========================================================================
  24++043A
  25++043A              X86_MEM_NGSPAGE_BASE EQU 4
  26++043A              X86_MEM_PAGE_COUNT   EQU 64
  27++043A
  28++043A              x86_mem_init:
  29++043A DB 0F                in      a, (GSCFG0)
  30++043C F6 09                or      M_NOROM | M_EXPAG
  31++043E D3 0F                out     (GSCFG0), a
  32++0440 C9                   ret
  33++0441
  34++0441              ; Convert x86_mem_addr into a Z80 pointer in C000-FFFF.
  35++0441              ; OUT: HL=window pointer, carry clear; carry set if address > FFFFF.
  36++0441              x86_mem_map_addr:
  37++0441 3A 89 06             ld      a, (x86_mem_addr+2)
  38++0444 FE 10                cp      #10
  39++0446 30 1E                jr      nc, .bad_address
  40++0448
  41++0448                      ; page = (physical >> 14) + 4
  42++0448                      ; page index = (high_nibble << 2) | (middle_byte >> 6)
  43++0448 87                   add     a, a
  44++0449 87                   add     a, a
  45++044A 47                   ld      b, a
  46++044B 3A 88 06             ld      a, (x86_mem_addr+1)
  47++044E 07                   rlca
  48++044F 07                   rlca
  49++0450 E6 03                and     #03
  50++0452 B0                   or      b
  51++0453 C6 04                add     a, X86_MEM_NGSPAGE_BASE
  52++0455
  53++0455                      ; MPAGEX uses ROL8(page,1); send ROR8(page,1).
  54++0455 0F                   rrca
  55++0456 D3 10                out     (MPAGEX), a
  56++0458
  57++0458                      ; Window C000-FFFF maps the selected 16 KiB physical page.
  58++0458 3A 88 06             ld      a, (x86_mem_addr+1)
  59++045B E6 3F                and     #3F
  60++045D F6 C0                or      #C0
  61++045F 67                   ld      h, a
  62++0460 3A 87 06             ld      a, (x86_mem_addr)
  63++0463 6F                   ld      l, a
  64++0464 B7                   or      a
  65++0465 C9                   ret
  66++0466
  67++0466              .bad_address:
  68++0466 37                   scf
  69++0467 C9                   ret
  70++0468
  71++0468              ; Read one byte. IN x86_mem_addr[0..2]. OUT A=byte, carry on invalid address.
  72++0468              x86_mem_read8:
  73++0468 CD 41 04             call    x86_mem_map_addr
  74++046B D8                   ret     c
  75++046C 7E                   ld      a, (hl)
  76++046D B7                   or      a
  77++046E C9                   ret
  78++046F
  79++046F              ; Write one byte. IN x86_mem_addr[0..2], x86_mem_value.
  80++046F              x86_mem_write8:
  81++046F CD 41 04             call    x86_mem_map_addr
  82++0472 D8                   ret     c
  83++0473 3A 8A 06             ld      a, (x86_mem_value)
  84++0476 77                   ld      (hl), a
  85++0477 B7                   or      a
  86++0478 C9                   ret
  87++0479
  88++0479              ; -------------------------------------------------------------------------
  89++0479              ; Destructive physical RAM mapping test.
  90++0479              ; Every page receives unique values at offsets 0000 and 3FFF. A second pass
  91++0479              ; verifies all 128 locations, detecting missing pages and page aliases.
  92++0479              ; -------------------------------------------------------------------------
  93++0479              x86_mem_test:
  94++0479 CD 3A 04             call    x86_mem_init
  95++047C AF                   xor     a
  96++047D 32 8B 06             ld      (x86_mem_page), a
  97++0480 32 8C 06             ld      (x86_mem_phase), a
  98++0483
  99++0483              .write_page_loop:
 100++0483 CD 4C 06             call    x86_mem_build_page_start
 101++0486 3A 8B 06             ld      a, (x86_mem_page)
 102++0489 EE 5A                xor     #5A
 103++048B 32 8A 06             ld      (x86_mem_value), a
 104++048E CD 6F 04             call    x86_mem_write8
 105++0491 DA 2A 06             jp      c, x86_mem_test_write_start_fail
 106++0494
 107++0494 CD 68 06             call    x86_mem_build_page_end

 109++049A EE A5                xor     #A5

 111++049F CD 6F 04             call    x86_mem_write8
 112++04A2 DA 2E 06             jp      c, x86_mem_test_write_end_fail
 113++04A5

 115++04A8 3C                   inc     a

 158++04F3 C9                   ret
 159++04F4
 160++04F4              ; -------------------------------------------------------------------------
 161++04F4              ; Segment:offset -> physical address.
 162++04F4              ; Uses x86_mem_segment and x86_mem_offset_work; result in x86_mem_addr.
 163++04F4              ; Physical address = ((segment << 4) + offset) & FFFFFh.
 164++04F4              ; -------------------------------------------------------------------------
 165++04F4              x86_mem_calc_phys:
 166++04F4                      ; Byte 0 = (segment low << 4) + offset low.
 167++04F4 3A 8D 06             ld      a, (x86_mem_segment)
 168++04F7 07                   rlca
 169++04F8 07                   rlca
 170++04F9 07                   rlca
 171++04FA 07                   rlca
 172++04FB 47                   ld      b, a
 173++04FC 3A 91 06             ld      a, (x86_mem_offset_work)
 174++04FF 80                   add     a, b
 175++0500 32 87 06             ld      (x86_mem_addr), a
 176++0503 3E 00                ld      a, 0
 177++0505 CE 00                adc     a, 0
 178++0507 32 95 06             ld      (x86_mem_carry), a
 179++050A
 180++050A                      ; Byte 1 base = (segment low >> 4) | (segment high << 4).
 181++050A 3A 8D 06             ld      a, (x86_mem_segment)
 182++050D 0F                   rrca
 183++050E 0F                   rrca
 184++050F 0F                   rrca

 191++0519 07                   rlca
 192++051A 07                   rlca
 193++051B B0                   or      b
 194++051C
 195++051C                      ; Add offset high to byte-1 base, then add byte-0 carry.
 196++051C                      ; The two carry-outs are mutually exclusive for byte addition.
 197++051C 47                   ld      b, a

 199++0520 80                   add     a, b
 200++0521 57                   ld      d, a

 203++0526 32 96 06             ld      (x86_mem_carry1), a   ; carry from base + offset high
 204++0529

 208++052E 80                   add     a, b

   1++0697              ; =========================================================================
   2++0697              ; PROJECT 124 — BIOS foundation / POST, stage 1
   3++0697              ;
   4++0697              ; Establishes the guest-visible low-memory data layout for a future 8086
   5++0697              ; real-mode BIOS. This stage does not implement executable BIOS ROM or INT
   6++0697              ; services. IVT is initialized to zero; vectors are installed only after the
   7++0697              ; CPU INT/IRET path and guest stack semantics are implemented.
   8++0697              ;
   9++0697              ; Physical memory profile:
  10++0697              ;   00000-003FF  IVT (256 entries, 4 bytes each)
  11++0697              ;   00400-004FF  BIOS Data Area (BDA)
  12++0697              ;   00500-9FFFF  conventional RAM profile (640 KiB)
  13++0697              ;   A0000-BFFFF  reserved video memory
  14++0697              ;   C0000-EFFFF  reserved option-ROM/device window
  15++0697              ;   F0000-FFFFF  reserved P124 BIOS ROM (not read-only yet)
  16++0697              ;
  17++0697              ; Entry: bios_post
  18++0697              ; Success: x86_exit_status=0, saved_x86_ax=#B105
  19++0697              ; Failure: x86_exit_status=7, saved_x86_ax=#B1xx
  20++0697              ; No DMA/IPC transport changes.
  21++0697              ; =========================================================================
  22++0697
  23++0697              BIOS_IVT_BASE             EQU #00000
  24++0697              BIOS_IVT_SIZE             EQU #00400
  25++0697              BIOS_BDA_BASE             EQU #00400
  26++0697              BIOS_BDA_SIZE             EQU #00100
  27++0697              BIOS_LOW_MEMORY_END       EQU #00500
  28++0697              BIOS_CONVENTIONAL_KB      EQU 640
  29++0697              BIOS_VIDEO_MODE_TEXT      EQU #03

   1++08E3              ; =========================================================================
   2++08E3              ; PROJECT 124 — 8086 16-bit ModR/M effective-address decoder foundation
   3++08E3              ;
   4++08E3              ; IN:  A = ModR/M byte, BC = pointer to next instruction byte.
   5++08E3              ; OUT: x86_ea_is_reg, x86_ea_reg, x86_ea_offset, x86_ea_segment.
   6++08E3              ;      BC advances past disp8/disp16. Carry set on truncated displacement.
   7++08E3              ;
   8++08E3              ; Register encoding for r/m and reg fields:
   9++08E3              ;   0 AX, 1 CX, 2 DX, 3 BX, 4 SP, 5 BP, 6 SI, 7 DI.
  10++08E3              ; Memory default segment is DS; forms containing BP use SS.
  11++08E3              ; mod=00,r/m=110 is direct [disp16] and uses DS.
  12++08E3              ; =========================================================================
  13++08E3
  14++08E3              x86_decode_modrm16:

  16++08E6 E6 C0                and     #C0

  18++08E9 07                   rlca

  20++08ED

  23++08F2 CB 3F                srl     a
  24++08F4 CB 3F                srl     a
  25++08F6 CB 3F                srl     a
  26++08F8 32 3E 0A             ld      (x86_modrm_reg), a
  27++08FB
  28++08FB 3A 3B 0A             ld      a, (x86_modrm_byte)
  29++08FE E6 07                and     7

   1++0A45              ; =========================================================================
   2++0A45              ; PROJECT 124 — minimal x86 interpreter core for NeoGS Z80
   3++0A45              ;
   4++0A45              ; Supported opcodes:
   5++0A45              ;   90       NOP
   6++0A45              ;   40       INC AX (8086 legacy one-byte register encoding)
   7++0A45              ;   B8 iw    MOV AX, imm16
   8++0A45              ;   F4       HLT (successful task termination)
   9++0A45              ;
  10++0A45              ; Unsupported opcode: status 2. Instruction budget exhausted: status 1.
  11++0A45              ; DMA error: status 3. Out-of-range/truncated instruction: status 4.
  12++0A45              ; x86_program_end is the exclusive logical address after the payload.
  13++0A45              ;
  14++0A45              ; Entry: BC = x86 IP in NeoGS Z80 logical address space; AX stored in
  15++0A45              ;        saved_z80_ax. Exit: saved_z80_ax and x86_exit_status are updated.
  16++0A45              ; =========================================================================
  17++0A45
  18++0A45              x86_execute_loop:
  19++0A45              x86_next_instruction:
  20++0A45                      ; Every opcode fetch must remain strictly below the exclusive end.
  21++0A45 2A 02 0D             ld      hl, (x86_program_end)
  22++0A48 B7                   or      a
  23++0A49 ED 42                sbc     hl, bc
  24++0A4B DA B8 0A             jp      c, x86_ip_out_of_range
  25++0A4E 7C                   ld      a, h
  26++0A4F B5                   or      l
  27++0A50 CA B8 0A             jp      z, x86_ip_out_of_range
  28++0A53
  29++0A53 2A 00 0D             ld      hl, (x86_steps_remaining)
  30++0A56 7C                   ld      a, h
  31++0A57 B5                   or      l
  32++0A58 20 09                jr      nz, .budget_ok

  35++0A5F 2A 2A 04             ld      hl, (saved_z80_ax)

  39++0A63 2B                   dec     hl
  40++0A64 22 00 0D             ld      (x86_steps_remaining), hl
  41++0A67
  42++0A67                      ; Fetch opcode and advance IP.
  43++0A67 0A                   ld      a, (bc)
  44++0A68 03                   inc     bc
  45++0A69
  46++0A69                      ; Resolve handler from 256-entry WORD table.
  47++0A69 6F                   ld      l, a
  48++0A6A 26 00                ld      h, 0
  49++0A6C 29                   add     hl, hl
  50++0A6D 11 00 0B             ld      de, x86_jump_table
  51++0A70 19                   add     hl, de
  52++0A71 5E                   ld      e, (hl)
  53++0A72 23                   inc     hl
  54++0A73 56                   ld      d, (hl)
  55++0A74
  56++0A74                      ; Restore AX in HL and jump to the selected handler. PUSH/RET is
  57++0A74                      ; the Z80's safe indirect-jump idiom; BC remains the virtual x86 IP.
  58++0A74 2A 2A 04             ld      hl, (saved_z80_ax)
  59++0A77 D5                   push    de
  60++0A78 C9                   ret
  61++0A79
  62++0A79              ; 90 — NOP
  63++0A79              x86_nop:

  65++0A7C C3 45 0A             jp      x86_next_instruction
  66++0A7F

  71++0A83 C3 45 0A             jp      x86_next_instruction
  72++0A86

  74++0A86              x86_mov_ax_imm16:

  76++0A86 2A 02 0D             ld      hl, (x86_program_end)

   1++0D05              ; =========================================================================
   2++0D05              ; PROJECT 124 — 8086 real-mode interrupt frame engine (Stage 2A)
   3++0D05              ;
   4++0D05              ; Implements the architectural memory/stack part of software INT and IRET.
   5++0D05              ; This module is deliberately separate from the legacy four-opcode fetch
   6++0D05              ; loop: opcode dispatch and execution of guest handler code are Stage 2B.
   7++0D05              ;
   8++0D05              ; x86_int_enter:
   9++0D05              ;   IN: A = interrupt vector number
  10++0D05              ;   Uses x86_reg_{ss,sp,cs,ip,flags}.
  11++0D05              ;   Push order: FLAGS, CS, IP; final SP points to saved IP.
  12++0D05              ;   Loads new IP:CS from IVT[vector], clears IF and TF.
  13++0D05              ;   OUT: carry clear on success, carry set on memory error.
  14++0D05              ;
  15++0D05              ; x86_iret_return:
  16++0D05              ;   Pops IP, CS, FLAGS; restores all three and advances SP by six.
  17++0D05              ;
  18++0D05              ; x86_int_self_test:
  19++0D05              ;   Installs a temporary vector 20h, validates the exact stack frame,
  20++0D05              ;   validates IF/TF clearing, executes IRET, validates restored state,
  21++0D05              ;   and removes the temporary vector.
  22++0D05              ;   Success: status=0, AX=1A01h. Failure: status=8, AX=1Axxh.
  23++0D05              ; =========================================================================
  24++0D05
  25++0D05              X86_INT_IF_TF_CLEAR_MASK EQU #FC
  26++0D05
  27++0D05              x86_int_enter:
  28++0D05 32 22 0F             ld      (x86_int_vector), a
  29++0D08
  30++0D08                      ; IVT entry address = vector * 4, physical segment zero.
  31++0D08 6F                   ld      l, a
  32++0D09 26 00                ld      h, 0
  33++0D0B 29                   add     hl, hl
  34++0D0C 29                   add     hl, hl
  35++0D0D 22 8F 06             ld      (x86_mem_offset), hl
  36++0D10 21 00 00             ld      hl, 0
  37++0D13 22 8D 06             ld      (x86_mem_segment), hl
  38++0D16 CD 52 05             call    x86_mem_read16
  39++0D19 D8                   ret     c
  40++0D1A 22 25 0F             ld      (x86_int_target_ip), hl
  41++0D1D
  42++0D1D 2A 8F 06             ld      hl, (x86_mem_offset)
  43++0D20 23                   inc     hl
  44++0D21 23                   inc     hl
  45++0D22 22 8F 06             ld      (x86_mem_offset), hl
  46++0D25 21 00 00             ld      hl, 0
  47++0D28 22 8D 06             ld      (x86_mem_segment), hl
  48++0D2B CD 52 05             call    x86_mem_read16
  49++0D2E D8                   ret     c
  50++0D2F 22 27 0F             ld      (x86_int_target_cs), hl
  51++0D32
  52++0D32                      ; 8086 interrupt entry pushes FLAGS, then CS, then return IP.
  53++0D32 ED 5B 39 0A          ld      de, (x86_reg_flags)
  54++0D36 CD 77 0D             call    x86_int_push_word
  55++0D39 D8                   ret     c
  56++0D3A ED 5B 31 0A          ld      de, (x86_reg_cs)
  57++0D3E CD 77 0D             call    x86_int_push_word
  58++0D41 D8                   ret     c
  59++0D42 ED 5B 37 0A          ld      de, (x86_reg_ip)
  60++0D46 CD 77 0D             call    x86_int_push_word
  61++0D49 D8                   ret     c
  62++0D4A
  63++0D4A                      ; IF (bit 9) and TF (bit 8) are cleared in the live FLAGS register.
  64++0D4A 3A 3A 0A             ld      a, (x86_reg_flags+1)
  65++0D4D E6 FC                and     X86_INT_IF_TF_CLEAR_MASK
  66++0D4F 32 3A 0A             ld      (x86_reg_flags+1), a
  67++0D52 2A 25 0F             ld      hl, (x86_int_target_ip)
  68++0D55 22 37 0A             ld      (x86_reg_ip), hl
  69++0D58 2A 27 0F             ld      hl, (x86_int_target_cs)
  70++0D5B 22 31 0A             ld      (x86_reg_cs), hl
  71++0D5E B7                   or      a
  72++0D5F C9                   ret
  73++0D60
  74++0D60              x86_iret_return:
  75++0D60 CD 97 0D             call    x86_int_pop_word
  76++0D63 D8                   ret     c
  77++0D64 22 37 0A             ld      (x86_reg_ip), hl
  78++0D67
  79++0D67 CD 97 0D             call    x86_int_pop_word
  80++0D6A D8                   ret     c
  81++0D6B 22 31 0A             ld      (x86_reg_cs), hl
  82++0D6E
  83++0D6E CD 97 0D             call    x86_int_pop_word
  84++0D71 D8                   ret     c
  85++0D72 22 39 0A             ld      (x86_reg_flags), hl
  86++0D75 B7                   or      a
  87++0D76 C9                   ret
  88++0D77
  89++0D77              ; IN: DE=word. Decrement guest SP by two, then write SS:SP.
  90++0D77              x86_int_push_word:
  91++0D77 ED 53 23 0F          ld      (x86_int_word), de
  92++0D7B 2A 27 0A             ld      hl, (x86_reg_sp)
  93++0D7E 2B                   dec     hl
  94++0D7F 2B                   dec     hl
  95++0D80 22 27 0A             ld      (x86_reg_sp), hl
  96++0D83 22 8F 06             ld      (x86_mem_offset), hl
  97++0D86 ED 5B 33 0A          ld      de, (x86_reg_ss)
  98++0D8A ED 53 8D 06          ld      (x86_mem_segment), de
  99++0D8E 2A 23 0F             ld      hl, (x86_int_word)
 100++0D91 22 93 06             ld      (x86_mem_word_value), hl
 101++0D94 C3 72 05             jp      x86_mem_write16
 102++0D97
 103++0D97              ; OUT: HL=word at SS:SP. SP advances only after a successful read.
 104++0D97              x86_int_pop_word:
 105++0D97 2A 27 0A             ld      hl, (x86_reg_sp)
 106++0D9A 22 8F 06             ld      (x86_mem_offset), hl
 107++0D9D ED 5B 33 0A          ld      de, (x86_reg_ss)
 108++0DA1 ED 53 8D 06          ld      (x86_mem_segment), de
 109++0DA5 CD 52 05             call    x86_mem_read16
 110++0DA8 D8                   ret     c
 111++0DA9 E5                   push    hl
 112++0DAA 2A 27 0A             ld      hl, (x86_reg_sp)
 113++0DAD 23                   inc     hl
 114++0DAE 23                   inc     hl
 115++0DAF 22 27 0A             ld      (x86_reg_sp), hl
 116++0DB2 E1                   pop     hl
 117++0DB3 B7                   or      a
 118++0DB4 C9                   ret

 121++0DB5              ; Deterministic architectural test. The test does not execute guest handler
 122++0DB5              ; instructions; it validates the real-mode entry/return frame primitives.

 124++0DB5              x86_int_self_test:

   1++0F29              ; =========================================================================
   2++0F29              ; PROJECT 124 — 8086 real-mode fetch/execute core, Stage 2B
   3++0F29              ;
   4++0F29              ; This bounded executable slice runs guest instructions through CS:IP and
   5++0F29              ; the physical-memory backend. Supported: IN/OUT byte/word, NOP, register-direct 8/16-bit
   6++0F29              ; arithmetic and logical subsets, PUSHF, INC/DEC r16, PUSH/POP r16,
   7++0F29              ; near/far CALL and RET, far JMP, MOV r8/r16,imm, MOV r/m8/r/m16 register-memory forms,
   8++0F29              ; short Jcc, LOOP/JCXZ, JMP rel8, HLT, INT imm8, INT3, IRET.
   9++0F29              ; Arithmetic and 8/16-bit logical memory ModR/M operands support segment overrides.
  10++0F29              ; It is intentionally not a general 8086 emulator.
  11++0F29              ;
  12++0F29              ; x86_real_mode_test installs a guest program at 0000:5002 and an interrupt
  13++0F29              ; handler at 0000:5100. The guest loads all eight general registers with
  14++0F29              ; MOV r16,imm16, invokes INT 20h, then resumes at HLT after handler IRET.
  15++0F29              ; The test checks all MOV destinations, interrupt return, FLAGS and SP.
  16++0F29              ; Success: status=0, AX=1A02h. Failures: status=9/11 with stage-specific AX codes.
  17++0F29              ; =========================================================================
  18++0F29

   2++1903              ; PROJECT 124 — 8086 real-mode 16-bit ALU (register and memory ModR/M)
   3++1903              ;
   4++1903              ; Supported 16-bit ModR/M forms:
   5++1903              ; ADD 01/03, ADC 11/13, SBB 19/1B, SUB 29/2B, CMP 39/3B.
   6++1903              ; Register and memory operands are supported in both directions. Memory
   7++1903              ; effective addresses and segment overrides use the shared MOV decoder.
   8++1903              ; 8-bit arithmetic and logical memory forms remain unsupported.

   4++2A2D              ; Implements 88h/89h/8Ah/8Bh:

   9++2A2D              ;   [BX+SI], [BX+DI], [BP+SI], [BP+DI], [SI], [DI], [BP], [BX]

   1++3027              ; =========================================================================
   2++3027              ; PROJECT124 — 8086 guest I/O port bus and IN/OUT instructions
   3++3027              ;
   4++3027              ; Implements 8086 opcodes:
   5++3027              ;   E4/E5  IN  AL/AX, imm8
   6++3027              ;   E6/E7  OUT imm8, AL/AX
   7++3027              ;   EC/ED  IN  AL/AX, DX
   8++3027              ;   EE/EF  OUT DX, AL/AX
   9++3027              ;
  10++3027              ; Guest I/O is deliberately isolated from physical NeoGS/Z80 ports.
  11++3027              ; The initial backend exposes one deterministic 16-bit test register at
  12++3027              ; guest port 00E0h. Byte access addresses its low lane; word access reads or
  13++3027              ; writes the complete register. Unmapped reads return FFh/FFFFh and writes
  14++3027              ; are ignored until actual PC/XT devices are added.
  15++3027              ; =========================================================================

   1++3A48              ; =========================================================================
   2++3A48              ; PROJECT 124 — BIOS INT 10h text services, Stage 3A
   3++3A48              ;
   4++3A48              ; Project text-console profile: 42 columns x 12 rows, 6x8 glyphs.
   5++3A48              ; Implemented functions:
   6++3A48              ;   AH=00h set mode 03h (project 42x12 text profile)
   7++3A48              ;   AH=0Fh get mode (AL=03h, AH=42, BH=0)
   8++3A48              ;   AH=02h set cursor (BH=0, DH=row, DL=column)
   9++3A48              ;   AH=03h get cursor (DH=row, DL=column)
  10++3A48              ;   AH=0Eh teletype output (AL=character, BH=0)
  11++3A48              ;
  12++3A48              ; INT 10h enters through the normal IVT/stack frame. The BIOS service itself
  13++3A48              ; is a firmware trap, then returns through the normal IRET primitive.
  14++3A48              ; Host stream protocol over DBIT/GSDAT:
  15++3A48              ;   nonzero byte = teletype character
  16++3A48              ;   00 00        = clear the lower debug console
  17++3A48              ;   00 01 row col= set lower debug console cursor
  18++3A48              ; =========================================================================
  19++3A48
  20++3A48              BIOS_VIDEO_COLUMNS EQU 42
  21++3A48              BIOS_VIDEO_ROWS    EQU 12
  22++3A48              BIOS_VIDEO_MODE    EQU #03
  23++3A48

  17  3E86              ; GS105a #14 writes the executable bootstrap into fixed RAM $4000-$7FFF.
  18  3E86                      ORG     $4000
  19  4000                      INCLUDE "bootstrap.asm"
# file opened: bootstrap.asm
   1+ 4000              ; =========================================================================
   2+ 4000              ; PROJECT 124 — first-stage NeoGS executable bootstrap
   3+ 4000              ;
   4+ 4000              ; GS105a command #14 (RECEIVE BLOCK) loads this raw Z80 image into fixed RAM
   5+ 4000              ; at $4000. GS105a command #13 (JUMP TO ADDRESS) enters it at $4000.
   6+ 4000              ;
   7+ 4000              ; The bootstrap maps RAM over the low ROM window, receives a 16-bit firmware
   8+ 4000              ; length and exactly that many bytes through the documented ZX data channel,
   9+ 4000              ; stores the custom firmware at RAM address $0000, then jumps to $0000.
  10+ 4000              ;
  11+ 4000              ; It executes from fixed RAM window $4000-$7FFF. Its stack is moved to
  12+ 4000              ; reserved $F000-$FFFF after the extended RAM windows are configured.
  13+ 4000              ; =========================================================================
  14+ 4000
  15+ 4000              neogs_bootstrap_start:
  16+ 4000 F3                   di
  17+ 4001 31 FF 3F             ld      sp, #3FFF
  18+ 4004
  19+ 4004                      ; Enable writable RAM-over-ROM and independent WIN2/WIN3 paging.
  20+ 4004 DB 0F                in      a, (GSCFG0)
  21+ 4006 E6 FD                and     #FD                    ; clear RAMRO: all target RAM must be writable
  22+ 4008 F6 01                or      M_NOROM                ; map RAM over the stock ROM at $0000
  23+ 400A F6 08                or      M_EXPAG                ; MPAG and MPAGEX independently select WIN2/WIN3
  24+ 400C D3 0F                out     (GSCFG0), a
  25+ 400E
  26+ 400E                      ; Physical 16-KiB page map used by the 48-KiB firmware layout:
  27+ 400E                      ; WIN0 $0000-$3FFF = fixed RAM page 0
  28+ 400E                      ; WIN1 $4000-$7FFF = fixed RAM page 3 (includes untouched DMA $5000-$7FFF)
  29+ 400E                      ; WIN2 $8000-$BFFF = RAM page 1; ROL8 encoding of page 1 is $80
  30+ 400E                      ; WIN3 $C000-$FFFF = RAM page 2; ROL8 encoding of page 2 is $01
  31+ 400E 3E 80                ld      a, #80
  32+ 4010 D3 00                out     (MPAG), a
  33+ 4012 3E 01                ld      a, #01
  34+ 4014 D3 10                out     (MPAGEX), a
  35+ 4016
  36+ 4016                      ; Stack lives in the reserved $F000-$FFFF part of WIN3. This keeps it
  37+ 4016                      ; safe while the bootstrap fills the low and upper firmware windows.
  38+ 4016 31 FE FF             ld      sp, #FFFE
  39+ 4019
  40+ 4019                      ; Tell Spectrum that the tiny loader is running and waiting for length.
  41+ 4019 3E A1                ld      a, NGS_BOOTSTRAP_READY_ACK
  42+ 401B CD 98 40             call    bootstrap_send_data_byte
  43+ 401E
  44+ 401E                      ; Host sends length low byte, then high byte.
  45+ 401E CD 84 40             call    bootstrap_receive_data_byte
  46+ 4021 5F                   ld      e, a
  47+ 4022 CD 84 40             call    bootstrap_receive_data_byte
  48+ 4025 57                   ld      d, a
  49+ 4026
  50+ 4026                      ; Reject zero length and any image larger than the low 16-KiB window ($4000).
  51+ 4026 7A                   ld      a, d
  52+ 4027 B3                   or      e
  53+ 4028 28 22                jr      z, bootstrap_error
  54+ 402A 7A                   ld      a, d
  55+ 402B FE 40                cp      #40
  56+ 402D 38 06                jr      c, bootstrap_length_ok
  57+ 402F 20 1B                jr      nz, bootstrap_error
  58+ 4031 7B                   ld      a, e
  59+ 4032 B7                   or      a
  60+ 4033 20 17                jr      nz, bootstrap_error
  61+ 4035
  62+ 4035              bootstrap_length_ok:
  63+ 4035                      ; Explicitly acknowledge that the length is accepted before the host
  64+ 4035                      ; starts streaming the firmware payload.
  65+ 4035 3E A3                ld      a, NGS_BOOTSTRAP_LENGTH_ACK
  66+ 4037 CD 98 40             call    bootstrap_send_data_byte
  67+ 403A 21 00 00             ld      hl, 0
  68+ 403D
  69+ 403D              bootstrap_copy_loop:
  70+ 403D 7A                   ld      a, d
  71+ 403E B3                   or      e
  72+ 403F 28 08                jr      z, bootstrap_start_firmware
  73+ 4041 CD 84 40             call    bootstrap_receive_data_byte
  74+ 4044 77                   ld      (hl), a
  75+ 4045 23                   inc     hl
  76+ 4046 1B                   dec     de
  77+ 4047 18 F4                jr      bootstrap_copy_loop
  78+ 4049
  79+ 4049              bootstrap_start_firmware:
  80+ 4049 C3 00 00             jp      0
  81+ 404C
  82+ 404C              bootstrap_error:
  83+ 404C 3E E1                ld      a, NGS_BOOTSTRAP_ERROR_ACK
  84+ 404E CD 98 40             call    bootstrap_send_data_byte
  85+ 4051 C3 54 40             jp      bootstrap_halt
  86+ 4054
  87+ 4054              ; Terminal state used when the host stops responding or cannot consume data.
  88+ 4054              ; Never leave a bootstrap polling loop running forever with no timeout.
  89+ 4054              bootstrap_halt:
  90+ 4054 F3                   di
  91+ 4055              .bootstrap_halt_loop:
  92+ 4055 76                   halt
  93+ 4056 18 FD                jr      .bootstrap_halt_loop
  94+ 4058
  95+ 4058              ; Best-effort error ACK after a receive timeout. This path has its own
  96+ 4058              ; bounded waits and never calls bootstrap_send_data_byte recursively.
  97+ 4058              bootstrap_timeout:
  98+ 4058 3E E1                ld      a, NGS_BOOTSTRAP_ERROR_ACK
  99+ 405A 32 C3 40             ld      (bootstrap_tx_byte), a
 100+ 405D 01 FF FF             ld      bc, #FFFF
 101+ 4060              .timeout_wait_slot:
 102+ 4060 DB 04                in      a, (ZXSTAT)
 103+ 4062 CB 7F                bit     B_DBIT, a
 104+ 4064 28 08                jr      z, .timeout_slot_ready
 105+ 4066 0B                   dec     bc
 106+ 4067 78                   ld      a, b

  20  40C4                      ASSERT neogs_bootstrap_start = $4000
  21  40C4                      ASSERT neogs_bootstrap_end <= $7FFF
  22  40C4              ; The host keeps the first TR-DOS sector in its reusable #C000 buffer while
  23  40C4              ; it installs and starts this bootstrap. Header + bootstrap must fit in 256 bytes.
  24  40C4                      ASSERT neogs_bootstrap_end - neogs_bootstrap_start <= 254
  25  40C4                      SAVEBIN "neogs_bootstrap.bin", neogs_bootstrap_start, neogs_bootstrap_end - neogs_bootstrap_start
  26  40C4                      SAVEBIN "zx_main/neogs_bootstrap.bin", neogs_bootstrap_start, neogs_bootstrap_end - neogs_bootstrap_start
  27  40C4

  29  40C4              ; binaries have been written. It is the only NeoGS payload exported to TR-DOS.
